# Chris Wu's Job Board

Software engineering, data science, cyber security, hardware engineering, and fintech internships for **Winter 2027** and **Summer 2027** (plus a **Later terms** section for Fall 2027 and beyond), gathered from public community lists and company job boards. Only roles identified as internships or co-ops in Canada, the United States, EU member countries, the United Kingdom, or remote worldwide are included; full-time roles are excluded. The tables show listings posted in the last 7 days, newest first. Older listings and listings with no posted date are in [data/listings.csv](data/listings.csv). For Apply links that open in a new tab, use the [interactive listings page](https://chriswu0000.github.io/Job_Searcher/). All sources are scraped when a commit is pushed to `main`; individual sources can also be scraped manually.

### Requirement icons

- 🛂 Does not offer sponsorship
- 🇺🇸 Requires U.S. citizenship

Last updated: 2026-10-10 | Winter 2027: 359 | Summer 2027: 544 | Later terms: 26

## Sources

To refresh all sources, push a commit to `main`. To refresh one source manually, open the **Actions** tab, choose **Scrape source**, click **Run workflow**, and pick the source (or run `bun run listings <source-id>` locally).

| Source | Last scraped | Listings |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SimplifyJobs Summer 2027 | 2026-10-08 | 2311 |
| zshah101 internships | 2026-10-08 | 758 |
| aprameyak tech jobs | 2026-10-08 | 1718 |
| intern-list.com | 2026-10-08 | 281 |
| Ashby boards | 2026-10-08 | 274 |
| Greenhouse boards | 2026-10-08 | 842 |
| Lever boards | 2026-10-08 | 140 |
| SmartRecruiters boards | 2026-10-08 | 96 |
| Workable boards | 2026-10-08 | 24 |
| Pinpoint boards | 2026-10-06 | 0 |
| Gem boards | 2026-10-06 | 0 |
| Workday boards | 2026-10-08 | 841 |

- Download [data/listings.csv](data/listings.csv) for every scraped listing, including older ones and ones with no posted date.

- **Posted** is the date the employer or job board published the listing, not the date it was scraped. It is blank when the source does not provide one.

- **Pay** is shown only when the source or posting text states it.

- A listing can appear under several categories or in both terms.

- Categories and terms are inferred from titles, tags, and descriptions, so check the posting before applying.

- Citizenship and sponsorship indicators are inferred from source descriptions or explicit eligibility fields; listings without accessible descriptions may not show an indicator.

- Job boards (Ashby, Greenhouse, Lever, SmartRecruiters, Workable, Pinpoint, Gem, Workday) are discovered from the community lists, so scrape those first. The optional Chrome extension is described in [docs/EXTENSION.md](docs/EXTENSION.md).
